
# Rarity Settings and factors used for tweaking
@very_rare = 0.50
@rare = 0.70
@uncommon = 0.85
@default = 1

# Factor multiplying terraform modifier chances based on planet designations
@factor_designation = 5
@factor_designation_food = 0.5 # temporary tweak to reduce variance of many food modifiers


# switch "fixable" negative modifiers with their fixed variant
gpm_switch_fixable_modifiers = {
	optimize_memory
	# Thin Ozone Layer
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = gpm_thin_ozone_layer }
		remove_modifier = gpm_thin_ozone_layer
		add_modifier = { modifier = gpm_thin_ozone_layer_fixed multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
	}
	# Weak Magnetic Field
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = gpm_weak_magnetic_field_gas_giant }
		remove_modifier = gpm_weak_magnetic_field_gas_giant
		add_modifier = { modifier = weak_magnetic_field multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
	}
	# Strong Magnetic Field
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = gpm_strong_magnetic_field_gas_giant }
		remove_modifier = gpm_strong_magnetic_field_gas_giant
		add_modifier = { modifier = strong_magnetic_field multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier_inverse }
	}
	# Mushroom Forest
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = gpm_mushroom_forest_toxic_world }
		remove_modifier = gpm_mushroom_forest_toxic_world
		add_modifier = { modifier = gpm_mushroom_forest multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
	}
	# Salt Flats
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = gpm_salt_flats }
		remove_modifier = gpm_salt_flats
		add_modifier = { modifier = gpm_salt_flats_terraform multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
	}
	# Subterranean Ecosystem
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = gpm_subterranean_eco_system }
		remove_modifier = gpm_subterranean_eco_system
		add_modifier = { modifier = gpm_subterranean_eco_system_terraform multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
	}
	# Abundant Natural Radioactivity
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = gpm_abundant_natural_radioactivity }
		remove_modifier = gpm_abundant_natural_radioactivity
		add_modifier = { modifier = gpm_abundant_natural_radioactivity_terraform multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier_inverse }
	}
	# Toxic Flora Fauna
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = gpm_toxic_flora_fauna }
		remove_modifier = gpm_toxic_flora_fauna
		add_modifier = { modifier = gpm_toxic_flora_fauna_terraform multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
	}
}

# Dissapearing deposits catcher
# Reapply deposits that dissapear, these are always kept
gpm_reapply_deposits_on_planet_class_change = {
	optimize_memory
	# unique deposits based on planet modifiers
	gpm_roll_planetary_features_unique = yes
	
	# relic world deposits
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = has_gpm_d_relic_tank_factory
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_relic_tank_factory }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_relic_tank_factory
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = has_gpm_d_relic_mech_factory
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_relic_mech_factory }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_relic_mech_factory
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = has_gpm_d_relic_power_armor_factory
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_relic_power_armor_factory }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_relic_power_armor_factory
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = has_gpm_d_relic_ship_factory
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_relic_ship_factory }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_relic_ship_factory
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = has_gpm_d_relic_reactor_factory
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_relic_reactor_factory }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_relic_reactor_factory
	}
	
	# planet wonders
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_gargantuan_sinkhole_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_gargantuan_sinkhole }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_gargantuan_sinkhole
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_titanic_cave_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_titanic_cave }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_titanic_cave
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_abandoned_stargate_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_abandoned_stargate }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_abandoned_stargate
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_bioluminescent_forest_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_bioluminescent_forest }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_bioluminescent_forest
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_land_reefs_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_land_reefs }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_land_reefs
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_lava_lake_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_lava_lake }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_lava_lake
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_Collapsed_habitat_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_Collapsed_habitat }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_Collapsed_habitat
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_ruined_arcology_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_ruined_arcology }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_ruined_arcology
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_discharging_field_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_discharging_field }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_discharging_field
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_giant_trees_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_giant_trees }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_giant_trees
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_crystal_jungle_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_crystal_jungle }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_crystal_jungle
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_floating_islands_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_floating_islands }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_floating_islands
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_amoeba_sea_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_amoeba_sea }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_amoeba_sea
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_glass_towers_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_glass_towers }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_glass_towers
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_howling_peaks_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_howling_peaks }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_howling_peaks
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_mega_fungi_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_mega_fungi }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_mega_fungi
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_energy_storm_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_energy_storm }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_energy_storm
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_planet_flag = is_gpm_d_forgotten_deposit_world
			NOT = { has_deposit = gpm_d_forgotten_deposit }
		}
		add_deposit = gpm_d_forgotten_deposit
	}
}

# Spawn a single terraform modifier matching hot dry worlds
gpm_roll_terraform_modifier_hot_dry = {
	optimize_memory
	random_list = {
		100 = {
			add_modifier = { modifier = "gpm_extinct_species" multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
			# Unique deposit
			random_list = {
				10 = { add_deposit = gpm_d_small_wildlife }
				10 = { add_deposit = gpm_d_massive_wildlife }
			}
			# Not with these modifiers
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_modifier = "gpm_extinct_species" }
			# Rarity
			# modifier = { factor = @default }
			# modifier = { factor = @uncommon }
			# modifier = { factor = @rare }
			modifier = { factor = @very_rare }
			# TWeaks #
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_generator_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_mining_designation = yes }
			modifier = { factor = @factor_designation_food has_farming_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_research_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_unity_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_trade_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_industrial_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_foundry_designation = yes }
		}
		100 = {
			add_modifier = { modifier = "gpm_hyper_complex_biome_terraform" multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
			# Unique deposit
			random_list = {
				10 = { add_deposit = gpm_d_small_wildlife }
				10 = { add_deposit = gpm_d_massive_wildlife }
			}
			# Not with these modifiers
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_modifier = "gpm_hyper_complex_biome_terraform" }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_modifier = "gpm_hyper_complex_biome" }
			# Rarity
			# modifier = { factor = @default }

			modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_research_designation = yes }

			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_trade_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_industrial_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_foundry_designation = yes }
		}
		100 = {
			add_modifier = { modifier = "gpm_dense_atmospheres_terraform" multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier_inverse }
			# Unique deposit
			if = {
				limit = { gpm_is_planet_class_hot_wet = yes }
				add_deposit = gpm_d_planetwide_jungles
			}
			if = {
				limit = { gpm_is_planet_class_hot_wet = no }
				add_deposit = gpm_d_planetwide_forest
			}
			# Not with these modifiers
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_modifier = "gpm_dense_atmospheres_terraform" }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_modifier = "gpm_dense_atmospheres" }
			# Rarity
			# modifier = { factor = @default }

			# Unique deposit
			add_deposit = gpm_d_dense_metal_veins

# Spawn a single terraform modifier matching toxic worlds
gpm_roll_terraform_modifier_toxic_habitable = {
	optimize_memory
	random_list = {
		100 = {
			add_modifier = { modifier = "gpm_hyper_complex_biome_terraform" multiplier = gpm_planet_size_multiplier }
			# Not with these modifiers
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_modifier = "gpm_hyper_complex_biome_terraform" }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_modifier = "gpm_hyper_complex_biome" }
			# Rarity
			# modifier = { factor = @default }
			modifier = { factor = @uncommon }
			# modifier = { factor = @rare }
			# modifier = { factor = @very_rare }
			# TWeaks #
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_generator_designation = yes }
			# modifier = { factor = @factor_designation has_mining_designation = yes }

# Spawn a single terraform modifier matching habitat worlds
gpm_roll_terraform_modifier_habitat = {
	optimize_memory

}

# Spawn a single terraform modifier matching ring worlds
gpm_roll_terraform_modifier_ring_world = {
	optimize_memory

}
